```text
NATURE OF THE PROCEEDINGS 
Section 10 of the Railway Labor Act charges Emergency Boards 
with the duty to' ".investigate promptly the facts as to the dispute and 
make a report thereon to the President within 30 days from the date 
ofitscreation. '' 
As Boa.rd No. 160 commented: "While the Act does not specify the 
manner in. which the Board is to carry out its investigation, it seems 
clear that by providing for a report in 30 days the framers of,.the 
legislation must have had in contemplation a flexible, expeditious
```

### T357085a2a032576e04b3532f

```text
INTRODUCTION 
On September 24, 1964, the President of the United States~ pursuant 
to Section 10 of the Railway Labor Act s as amended, by Executive 
Order. 11180 created this Emergency Board No. 164 to investigate and 
report on the dispute between1 railroads of the country represented by 
the National Railway Labor Conference and the Eastern, Western and 
Southeastern Carriers' Conference Committees and the Brotherhood 
of Locomotive Firemen and Enginemen.
```

```text
Saturday, S eptem ber 26, 1964 FEDERAL REGISTER 13365
Executive Order 11180
CREATING AN EMERGENCY BOARD TO INVESTIGATE DISPUTES BE­
TWEEN THE CARRIERS REPRESENTED BY THE NATIONAL RAILWAY
LABOR.CONFERENCE AND CERTAIN OF THEIR EMPLOYEES
W H E R E A S  disputes exist between the carriers represented by the 
N ational R ailw ay L abor C onference, designated in L ist A  attached 
hereto and made a part h ereof, and certain o f  their em ployees repre­
sented by the B rotherhood o f L ocom otive Firem en and Enginem en, a 
labor organ ization ; and
W H E R E A S  these disputes have not h eretofore been adjusted under 
the provisions o f  the R ailw ay L abor A ct, as am ended; and
W H E R E A S  these disputes, in the judgm ent o f the N ational M edi­
ation B oard, threaten substantially to interrupt interstate com m erce 
to a degree such as to deprive the country o f essential transportation 
service:
N O W , T H E R E F O R E , by virtue o f the authority vested in me by 
Section 10 o f  the R ailw ay L abor A ct,' as amended (45 U .S.C . 160), I  
hereby create a board, to be appointed by me, to investigate these 
disputes. N o member o f the board shall be pecuniarily or otherwise 
interested in any organization o f railroad em ployees o r any carrier.
The board shall report its findings to the President w ith respect to 
the disputes w ithin th irty days from  the date o f this order.
A s provided by Section 10 o f the R ailw ay L abor A ct, as amended, 
from  this date and fo r  th irty days after the board has made its report 
to the President, no change, except by agreement, shall be made b y the 
carriers represented by the N ational R ailw ay L abor C onference, or by 
their em ployees, in  the conditions out o f w hich the disputes arose.
T he  W hite  H ouse,
September 24,1964-
```

```text
Presidential Documents
Title 3— THE PRESIDENT
Executive Order 11135
CREATING AN EMERGENCY BOARD TO INVESTIGATE DISPUTES BE­
TWEEN THE CARRIERS REPRESENTED BY THE EASTERN, WESTERN  
AND SOUTHEASTERN CARRIERS’ CONFERENCE COMMITTEES, AND  
CERTAIN OF THEIR EMPLOYEES
W H E R E A S  disputes exist between the carriers represented by the 
Eastern, Western and Southeastern Carriers’ Conference Committees, 
designated in List A  attached hereto and made a part hereof, and 
certain of their employees represented by the Brotherhood of Railroad 
Signalmen, a labor organization; and
W H E R E A S  these disputes have not heretofore been adjusted under 
the provisions of the Railway Labor Act, as amended; and
W H E R E A S  these disputes, in the judgment of the National Media­
tion Board, threaten substantially to interrupt interstate commerce 
to a degree such as to deprive the country of essential transportation 
service:
NO W , T H E R E F O R E , by virtue of the authority vested in me by 
Section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, as amended (4:5 U.S.C. 160), I  
hereby create; a board of three members, to be appointed by me, to 
investigate these disputes. No member of the board shall be pecuni­
arily or otherwise interested in any organization of railroad employees 
or any carrier.
The board shall report its findings to the President with respect to 
the disputes within thirty days from the date o f this order.
As provided by Section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, as amended, 
from this date and for thirty days after the board has made its report 
to the President, no change, except by agreement, shall be made by the 
carriers represented by the Eastern, Western and Southeastern 
Carriers’ Conference Committees, or by their employees, in the condi­
tions out of which the disputes arose.
L y n d o n B. J o h n s o n
T h e W h it e H ouse ,
January 3, 196 J.
```

```text
Thursday, August 20, 1964 FEDERAL REGISTER 11889
Executive Order 11169
CREATING AN EMERGENCY BOARD TO INVESTIGATE DISPUTES BE­
TWEEN THE CARRIERS REPRESENTED BY THE NATIONAL RAILWAY
LABOR CONFERENCE AND CERTAIN OF THEIR EMPLOYEES
W HEREAS disputes exist between the carriers represented by the 
National Railway Labor Conference, designated in List A attached 
hereto and made a part hereof, and certain of their employees rep­
resented by the Six Cooperating Railway Labor Organizations func­
tioning through the Railway Employes’  Department, AFL-CIO, 
labor organizations, designated in the List B attached hereto and made 
a part hereof; and
W HEREAS these disputes have not heretofore been adjusted under 
the provisions of the Railway Labor Act, as amended; and
W HEREAS these disputes, in the judgment of the National Medi­
ation Board, threaten substantially to interrupt interstate commerce 
to a degree siich as to deprive the country of essential transportation 
service:
NOW, THEREFORE, by virtue of the authority vested in me by 
Section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, as amended (45 U.S.C. 160), I 
hereby create a board, to be appointed by me, to investigate these 
disputes. No member of the board shall be pecuniarily or otherwise 
interested in any organization of railroad employees or any carrier.
The board shall report its findings to the President with respect to 
the disputes within thirty days from the date of this order.
As provided by Section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, as amended, 
from this date and for thirty days after the board has made its report 
to the President, no change, except by agreement, shall be made by 
the carriers represented by the National Railway Labor Conference, 
or by their employees, in the conditions out of which the disputes arose.
T he  W hite  H ouse,
August 18, 196Ji.
```

```text
Tuesday, March 23, 1965 FEDERAL REGISTER 3741
Executive Order 11206
INSPECTION OF INTEREST EQUALIZATION TAX RETURNS
By virtue of the authority vested in me by section 6103(a) of the  
Internal Revenue Code of 1954 (68A Stat. 753; 26 U.S.C. 6103(a )),  
as amended by section 3(c) of the Interest Equalization Tax Act  
(78 Stat. 844), it is hereby ordered that returns made in respect of  
the tax imposed by chapter 41 of such Code shall be open to inspec­
tion by certain classes of persons and State and Federal government  
establishments in accordance and upon compliance with the rules and  
regulations prescribed by the Secretary of the Treasury in Treasury  
Decision 6543, relating tor inspection and use of returns by such classes  
of persons and State and Federal government establishments, ap­
proved by the President on January 17,1961, the amendment thereto  
approved by the President on April 4, 1963, and the amendment  
thereto approved by me this date.
This order shall be effective upon its filing for publication in the  
F ederal  R egister .
L tndon  B. J ohnson
T h e W h it e H ouse ,
March 18, 1965.
[F.R. Doc. 65-3065; Filed, Mar. 22, 1965; 11:05 a.m.]

```

```text
Thursday , August 20, 1964 FEDERAL REGISTER 11893
Executive Order 11170
CREATING AN EMERGENCY BOARD TO INVESTIGATE DISPUTES BE­
TWEEN THE CARRIERS REPRESENTED BY THE NATIONAL RAILWAY
LABOR CONFERENCE AND CERTAIN OF THEIR EMPLOYEES
W HEREAS disputes exist between the carriers represented by the 
National Railway Labor Conference, designated in List A attached 
hereto and made a part thereof, and certain of their employees rep­
resented by the Five Cooperating Railway Labor Organizations, labor 
organizations, designated in List B attached hereto and made a part 
hereof; and
W HEREAS these disputes have not heretofore been adjusted under 
the provisions of the Railway Labor Act, as amended; and
W HEREAS these disputes, in the judgment of the National Medi­
ation Board, threaten substantially to interrupt interstate commerce 
to a degree such as to deprive the country of essential transportation 
service:
NOW, THEREFORE, by virtue of the authority vested in me by 
Section 10, of the Railway Labor Act, as amended (45 TJ.S.C. 160), 
I  hereby create a board to be appointed by me, to investigate these 
disputes. No member of the board shall be pecuniarily or otherwise 
interested in any organization of railroad employees or any carrier.
The board shall report its findings to the President with respect to 
the disputes within thirty days from the date of this order.
As provided by Section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, as amended, 
from this date and for thirty days after the board has made its report 
to the President, no change, except by agreement, shall be made by 
the carriers represented by the National Railway Labor Conference, or 
by their employees, in the conditions out of which the disputes arose.
T he  W hite  H ouse,
August 18, 196
L yndon  B. J ohnson
```

```text
THE PRESIDENT 21315
[3195-01]
Executive Order 12060 M ay 15,1978
Relating to Certain Positions in Levels IV and V of the Executive Schedule
By virtue of the authority vested in me by Section 5317 of'Title 5 of the  
United States Code, and as President of the United States of America, it is  
hereby ordered as follows:
Se c t io n 1. Section 1 of Executive Order No. 11861, as amended, placing  
certain positions in level IV of the Executive Schedule, is further amended by  
inserting in numerical sequence “(6) Assistant Attorney General, United States 
Attorneys and Trial Advocacy, Department of Justice.” and by deleting  
“Counselor to the Secretary for Congressional Affairs” in subsection (10) and 
inserting in lieu thereof, “Deputy Under Secretary for Regional Affairs”.
Se c. 2. Section 2 of Executive Order No. 11861, as amended, placing  
certain positions in level V of the Executive Schedule, is further amended by  
deleting “(13) Executive Director, Federal Personnel Management Systems  
Study, United States Civil Service Commission.”.
Sec.  3. Executive Order No. 11189 of November 23, 1964, Executive 
Order No. 11195 of January 30, 1965, and Executive Order No. 11995 of June 
8, 1977 are revoked.
T h e W h it e H o u s e, 
May 15, 1978.
[FR Doc. 78-13574 Filed 5-16-78; 10:14 am]
FEDERAL REGISTER, VOL. 43, N O. 96— WEDNESDAY, M A Y 17, 1978

```

```text
Presidential Docum ents
Title 3— THE PRESIDENT
Executive Order 11181
CREATING A BOARD OF INQUIRY TO REPORT ON CERTAIN LABOR
DISPUTES AFFECTING THE MARITIME INDUSTRY OF THE UNITED
STATES
W H E R E A S , there exist certain labor disputes between em ployers 
(o r  associations by w hich such em ployers are represented in collective 
bargaining conferences) who are (1 ) steamship com panies or who are 
engaged as operators or agents fo r  ships engaged in service from  or 
to  A tlan tic and G u lf Coast ports from  Searsport, M aine, to B row ns­
v ille, Texas, or from  or to other ports o f the U nited States or its terri­
tories or possessions, (2 ) contracting stevedores, (3 ) contracting ma­
rine carpenters, (4 ) lighterage operators, or (5 ) other em ployers en­
gaged in related or associated pier activities and certain o f their 
em ployees represented by the International Longshorem en’s A ssocia­
tion , A F L -C I O ; and
W H E R E A S , such disputes have resulted in a threatened strike 
w hich i f  perm itted to occur, w ill, in m y opinion, affect a substantial 
part o f the m aritim e industry, an industry engaged in trade, com ­
m erce, transportation, transm ission, or com m unication am ong the 
several States and w ith foreign  nations, and w hich threatened strike 
w ill, i f  perm itted to occur, im peril the national health and safety and 
affect the flow  and utilization o f necessary perishable products, in ­
clu din g food , fo r  heavily populated coastal, island, and insular areas;
N O W , T H E R E F O R E , by virtue o f  the authority vested in me by 
Section 206 o f the Labor-M anagem ent R elations A ct, 1947 (61 Stat. 
155; 29 U .S.C . 176), I  hereby create a B oard o f In qu iry, consisting o f 
H onorable H erbert Schm ertz, as Chairm an, H onorable James J . 
H ealy, and H onorable T heodore W . K heel, as Members, whom  I  
hereby appoint to inquire into the issues involved in such disputes.
T he B oard shall have pow ers and duties as set fo rth  in T itle I I  
o f  such A ct. T he B oard shall report to the President in accordance 
w ith the provisions o f Section 206 o f such A ct bn or before Thursday, 
O ctober 1,1964.
U pon  the submission o f its report, the B oard shall continue in 
existence to perform  such other functions as m ay be required under 
such A ct.
L y n d o n B . J oh n son
T h e W h it e H ouse,
September 30, 196J.
[F.R. Doc. 64-10067; Filed, Oct. 1,1964; 9 :3 3  a.m.]

```


```text
FEDERAL EXECUTIVE SALARY SCHEDULE
By virtue of the authority vested in me by subsection (g) of Section 
303 of the Government Employees Salary Reform Act of 1964, and as 
President of the United States, it is ordered as follows :
Section  1. Section 1 of Executive Order 11189 of November 23, 
1964, is amended by adding thereto the following :
(6) Chief of Protocol, Department of State.
Sec. 2. Section 2 of Executive Order 11189 of November 23,1964, is 
amended by adding thereto the following :
(15) Staff Director, Commission on Civil Rights.
(16) Assistant Director (Program Planning, Analysis, and Re­
search), Office of Economic Opportunity.
(17) General Counsel, Veterans Administration.
Sec. 3. This Order shall be effective immediately.
T h e W h ite H ouse,
January 30 ,  1965.
L yndon  B. J ohnson
[F.R. Doc. 65-1292 ; Filed, Feb. 3, 1965; 10:12 a.m.J

```

```text
Thursday , August 20, 1964 FEDERAL REGISTER 11885
Executive Order 11168
CREATING AN EMERGENCY BOARD TO INVESTIGATE DISPUTES BE­
TWEEN THE CARRIERS REPRESENTED BY THE NATIONAL RAILWAY  
LABOR CONFERENCE AND CERTAIN OF THEIR EMPLOYEES
W HEREAS disputes exist between the carriers represented by the 
National Railway Labor Conference, designated in List A attached 
hereto and made a part hereof, and certain of their employees rep­
resented by the Eleven Cooperating Railway Labor Organizations, 
labor organizations, designated in List B attached hereto and made a 
part hereof; and
W HEREAS these disputes have not heretofore been adjusted under 
the provisions of the Railway Labor Act, as amended; and
W HEREAS these disputes, in the judgment of the National Media­
tion Board, threaten substantially to interrupt interstate commerce 
to a degree such as to deprive the country of essential transportation 
service:
NOW, THEREFORE, by virtue of the authority vested in me by 
Section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, as amended (45 TJ.S.C. 160), 
I  hereby create a board, to be appointed by me, to investigate these 
disputés. No member of the board shall be pecuniarily or otherwise 
interested in any organization of railroad employees or any carrier. \
The board shall report its findings to the President with respect to 
the disputes within thirty days from the date of this order.
As provided by Section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, as amended, 
from this date and for thirty days after the board has made its report 
to the President, no change, except by agreement, shall be made by the 
carriers represented by the National Railway Labor Conference, or by 
their employees, in the conditions out of which the disputes arose.
T he  W hite  H ouse,
August 18 ,  1961^ .
```

```text
Tuesday , A p ril 6, 1965 FEDERAL REGISTER 4389
Executive Order 11213
INSPECTION OF CERTAIN INTEREST EQUALIZATION TAX INFORMA­
TION RETURNS BY THE BOARD OF GOVERNORS OF THE FEDERAL
RESERVE SYSTEM AND THE FEDERAL RESERVE BANKS
By virtue of the authority vested in me by section 6103(a) of the 
Internal Revenue Code of 1954 (68A Stat. 753; 26 U.S.C. 6103(a)), 
as amended by section 3(c) of the Interest Equalization Tax Act 
(Pub. L. 88-563, 78 Stat.844), it is hereby ordered that any informa­
tion return made by a commercial bank under the Interest Equaliza­
tion Tax Act shall be open to inspection by the Board of Governors 
of the Federal Reserve System and the Federal Reserve Banks. Such 
inspection shall be in accordance and upon compliance with the rules 
and regulations prescribed by the Secretary of the Treasury in a 
Treasury decision, relating to inspection of certain interest equaliza­
tion tax information returns by the Board of Governors of the Federal 
Reserve System and the Federal Reserve Banks, approved by me this 
date.
Executive Order 11176 of September 3, 1964, is hereby superseded.
This order shall be effective upon its filing for publication in the 
F ederal  R egister .
L y n d o n B. J o h n so n
T h e W h it e H ouse ,
A p ril 2, 1965.
```

### Tfa94823a99efa0564f09b4f1

Text hash 2f72fa0237a631ed99f7621104afffecf4cf5da1649db633b6a19cd409f949fc; absolute characters 6178–6702; files: jobs/source-ladder/output/texts/queue_EO_11170_Actual_report_date_of_the_board_created_by_EO_11170_Presidential_Emergency_Board_Rail_759d37f9e4.txt

```text
NATURE OF THE PROCEEDINGS 
Section 10 of the Railway Labor Act charges Emergency Boards 
with the duty to' ".investigate promptly the facts as to the dispute and 
make a report thereon to the President within 30 days from the date 
ofitscreation. '' 
As Boa.rd No. 160 commented: "While the Act does not specify the 
manner in. which the Board is to carry out its investigation, it seems 
clear that by providing for a report in 30 days the framers of,.the 
legislation must have had in contemplation a flexible, expeditious
```

### Tfe5ad0db5372895d08e9d3e2

Text hash 0e28a414f5b242a5b69ce94dae3befb0d4153aefc1be83410a98e5bc70e5753a; absolute characters 7175–7442; files: jobs/source-ladder/output/texts/queue_EO_11181_Actual_report_date_of_the_board_created_by_EO_11181_Presidential_Emergency_Board_Rail_d23e933ec6.txt

```text
The result is that no Board in recent years has been able to complete 
its report within the statutory 'period', and if both the, railroads and 
the unions are given a free hand in" these hearings: their testimony 
could easily occupy 50 to 60 full days. of hearings.
```

### Tfe90d972edfa4120ab10349d

Text hash a2af9da1b7436eaf4cf88b1e36a6bcb41ed1ba7aec2e63e440dc97d0dbafc2e9; absolute characters 1398–1744; files: jobs/clause-drafts/output/texts/cd-job-fr-originals-11169-314976d778.txt

```text
As provided by Section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, as amended, 
from this date and for thirty days after the board has made its report 
to the President, no change, except by agreement, shall be made by 
the carriers represented by the National Railway Labor Conference, 
or by their employees, in the conditions out of which the disputes arose.
```

Establish occurrence from evidence and explain its effect on this clause."],"scope_id":"EO:11168#whole","state":"requires_clause_specific_research"}],"existing_lifecycle_questions_notice":"Current dossier research prompts, not new gap findings or overrides of prior claim/review conclusions.","new_legal_gaps_invented":[],"prior_differences_require_scope_source_proposition_review":true},"selection_and_checkpoint_records":[],"state":"prior_work_located","status_holds":[],"status_research":[],"unattached_status_reviews":[],"unfinished_work":[],"versions":[{"claim_sha256":"95aa10ec22f2ce977558af0c99ba08184f02eb6f29f5b38e596648b63e1726d7","current_canonical":true,"locations":[{"file_sha256":"3020d4aeb53b8d8d20f485530423b36304bd6e5d03a8c0a3ec7c206846aabf3b","path":"research/readiness/claims/claude-2026-10-31-apollo-g07.jsonl","pointer":"line:15"}],"reported_material_gaps":[],"research_rationale":"Identity: the held Federal Register text (29 FR 11885) prints Executive Order 11168 as an order of the President (Lyndon B. Johnson) dated 1964-08-18; the number, subject and date agree with the census register row (subject: Creating an emergency board to investigate disputes between the carriers represented by the National Railway Labor Conference and certain of their employees). I read the whole clause and the signature block. Inventory: the dossier's single whole-order clause covers the recitals and every operative sentence; nothing outside it is a command (any attached lists of carriers or unions identify parties and are not commands). The one operative clause creates an emergency board under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, 45 U.S.C. 160, to investigate the dispute involving the carriers represented by the National Railway Labor Conference and certain of their employees; it bars board members from pecuniary or other interest in any carrier or employee organization; it directs the board to report its findings to the President within thirty days from the date of the order; and it recites the statutory standstill: from the date of the order and for thirty days after the board reports, no change except by agreement is to be made in the conditions out of which the dispute arose. Ending: EO 12553 of February 25, 1986, titled \"Revocation of Various Executive Orders\", orders that the listed Executive Orders, and \"any amendments thereto, are revoked:\" and its table lists this order by number and subject on one line: \"11168 Creating an emergency board to investigate railroad labor disputes\". EO 12553 contains no effective-date wording (the held text was searched for effective, take effect, shall become, except, notwithstanding, nothing in, provided that and saving, with no hit outside table subjects); it is headed as an order of February 25, 1986 and signed that day, so the date is read as 1986-02-25, the signing date. The alternative reading is the Federal Register publication date, 1986-03-03 (Monday, March 3, 1986, 51 FR 7237); no later date is supported by the text. The packet holds no located acting-provision record for this pair (the dossier lists the target provision as not located), so the end is cited to the complete held text of EO 12553 (Federal Register copy, 51 FR 7237), which contains both the enacting paragraph and the table row quoted above; I read the whole text and the row is present on its own line. The thirty-day report deadline and the standstill that runs to thirty days after the report are periods measured by the order's own terms. Under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on report deadlines, neither the report nor the end of the standstill ends the order; each is a spent fact, and the order stayed on the books until it was expressly revoked. The order was functionally spent once the board had reported and the standstill had run, but this claim assigns no date to that point because no report date is cited from a registered source here; a board report held as a lead-grade Government printing is not cited, and a dated transmittal letter is not proof of receipt or of standstill expiry. Earlier candidates: a search of the held order texts for citations of this order by number found no later order that revokes, amends or extends it; the Office of the Federal Register catalog notes name EO 12553 as the revoking order. Savings: EO 12553 has no savings clause or exception, and the order created no continuing right or obligation outside its own terms. Searches selected after reading their coverage notes (all three complete, no coverage warning): U.S. Code release point 119-121 (no hit), eCFR as of 2026-10-14 (no hit), and the Federal Register catalog disposition notes (2 machine hits, the revocation note on this order and the matching note on EO 12553).\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Own-terms periods (report deadline and standstill) are not ends under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21. No held source shows an earlier revocation, supersession, expiry or termination. The first ending shown by held evidence is the express revocation by number in EO 12553, effective 1986-02-25. Later orders that merely recite or list this order would not be ends; none was found that does more than EO 12553.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"The order has no command independent of the board: the standstill is recited from the statute and depends on the board's creation and report. No other command in the order, and no other order that incorporates it, was found in the held texts.\",\"savings_check\":\"EO 12553 has no savings clause or exception (searched as described); the board's report and any settlement or later legislation are not obligations created by this order and are not claimed as surviving effects.\",\"scope_check\":\"Single clause, whole order. The dossier's inventory spans the recitals, the creation of the board, the report command and the statutory standstill recital to the signature; I confirm it as complete. EO 12553 revokes the whole order (it lists the order itself and its amendments), so no part of the order is left outside the ending.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:11168#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:11168#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"a2ece72d79a90b65cd6f402f6d8286c374d471992c14beaa073f84594e094058","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11168.txt","sha256":"a2ece72d79a90b65cd6f402f6d8286c374d471992c14beaa073f84594e094058","state":"match"},"sha256":"314976d77803203796dd4e12e7d3f12a6bca8f56ac44e37dffb1dd61e9f82b4e","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11168.txt","text_sha256":"a2ece72d79a90b65cd6f402f6d8286c374d471992c14beaa073f84594e094058"}]}]}

Johnson) dated 1964-08-18; the number, subject and date agree with the census register row (subject: Creating an emergency board to investigate disputes between the carriers represented by the National Railway Labor Conference and certain of their employees). I read the whole clause and the signature block. Inventory: the dossier's single whole-order clause covers the recitals and every operative sentence; nothing outside it is a command (any attached lists of carriers or unions identify parties and are not commands). The one operative clause creates an emergency board under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, 45 U.S.C. 160, to investigate the dispute involving the carriers represented by the National Railway Labor Conference and certain of their employees; it bars board members from pecuniary or other interest in any carrier or employee organization; it directs the board to report its findings to the President within thirty days from the date of the order; and it recites the statutory standstill: from the date of the order and for thirty days after the board reports, no change except by agreement is to be made in the conditions out of which the dispute arose. Ending: EO 12553 of February 25, 1986, titled \"Revocation of Various Executive Orders\", orders that the listed Executive Orders, and \"any amendments thereto, are revoked:\" and its table lists this order by number and subject on one line: \"11169 Creating an emergency board to investigate railroad labor disputes\". EO 12553 contains no effective-date wording (the held text was searched for effective, take effect, shall become, except, notwithstanding, nothing in, provided that and saving, with no hit outside table subjects); it is headed as an order of February 25, 1986 and signed that day, so the date is read as 1986-02-25, the signing date. The alternative reading is the Federal Register publication date, 1986-03-03 (Monday, March 3, 1986, 51 FR 7237); no later date is supported by the text. The packet holds no located acting-provision record for this pair (the dossier lists the target provision as not located), so the end is cited to the complete held text of EO 12553 (Federal Register copy, 51 FR 7237), which contains both the enacting paragraph and the table row quoted above; I read the whole text and the row is present on its own line. The thirty-day report deadline and the standstill that runs to thirty days after the report are periods measured by the order's own terms. Under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on report deadlines, neither the report nor the end of the standstill ends the order; each is a spent fact, and the order stayed on the books until it was expressly revoked. The order was functionally spent once the board had reported and the standstill had run, but this claim assigns no date to that point because no report date is cited from a registered source here; a board report held as a lead-grade Government printing is not cited, and a dated transmittal letter is not proof of receipt or of standstill expiry. Earlier candidates: a search of the held order texts for citations of this order by number found no later order that revokes, amends or extends it; the Office of the Federal Register catalog notes name EO 12553 as the revoking order. Savings: EO 12553 has no savings clause or exception, and the order created no continuing right or obligation outside its own terms. Searches selected after reading their coverage notes (all three complete, no coverage warning): U.S. Code release point 119-121 (no hit), eCFR as of 2026-10-14 (no hit), and the Federal Register catalog disposition notes (2 machine hits, the revocation note on this order and the matching note on EO 12553).\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Own-terms periods (report deadline and standstill) are not ends under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21. No held source shows an earlier revocation, supersession, expiry or termination. The first ending shown by held evidence is the express revocation by number in EO 12553, effective 1986-02-25. Later orders that merely recite or list this order would not be ends; none was found that does more than EO 12553.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"The order has no command independent of the board: the standstill is recited from the statute and depends on the board's creation and report. No other command in the order, and no other order that incorporates it, was found in the held texts.\",\"savings_check\":\"EO 12553 has no savings clause or exception (searched as described); the board's report and any settlement or later legislation are not obligations created by this order and are not claimed as surviving effects.\",\"scope_check\":\"Single clause, whole order. The dossier's inventory spans the recitals, the creation of the board, the report command and the statutory standstill recital to the signature; I confirm it as complete. EO 12553 revokes the whole order (it lists the order itself and its amendments), so no part of the order is left outside the ending.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:11169#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:11169#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"b19332836989f1f050156ee1541b6e85fdab2a7cdf8eb3ef666917747e1d5d70","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11169.txt","sha256":"b19332836989f1f050156ee1541b6e85fdab2a7cdf8eb3ef666917747e1d5d70","state":"match"},"sha256":"314976d77803203796dd4e12e7d3f12a6bca8f56ac44e37dffb1dd61e9f82b4e","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11169.txt","text_sha256":"b19332836989f1f050156ee1541b6e85fdab2a7cdf8eb3ef666917747e1d5d70"}]}]}

I read the full held Federal Register text (jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11170.txt, 29 FR 11893, PDF pages 13-16) and confirmed that its printed number, title and signature date match the census register row. The order has one operative paragraph with no numbered sections and no stated end date, so the dossier's single whole-order clause is the complete inventory (any carrier list the order attaches is an incorporated list, not a separate command). EO 12553 (signed 1986-02-25; held text jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt) revokes the order by number in its table. The first end is that express revocation. The report deadline, the report itself and the end of the standstill are not ends under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on report deadlines, and no held reviewed source establishes the dates on which the board reported or the standstill ran out. Bounded search read for this order: the dossier's three machine searches (U.S. Code release point 119-121 through 2026-10-09; eCFR and GovInfo CFR bulk through 2026-10-14; Federal Register catalog disposition notes and operative sentences of 17,521 saved order texts), after reading their coverage notes, which carry no coverage warning; the Cruden by-number scan of held order texts in the packet's LOOKUPS.md; and a full read of EO 12553 (held FR text, 33,227 characters).\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Own terms: the order states a report deadline (\\\"within thirty days from the date of this order\\\") and a standstill running (\\\"for thirty days after the board has made its report\\\"). A report deadline is a deadline, not an end, and the standstill is a spent fact whose date no held source fixes; neither is recorded as an end. The order states no date on which it ends. Held later instruments: the by-number scan lists no held later order that cites this order other than EO 12553, which names it in its revocation table; the Federal Register catalog note records the EO 12553 revocation and the U.S. Code and eCFR searches return no hit. No unnamed statute or order was found that would be an earlier candidate end beyond section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, the statute the order itself invokes.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Independent commands: the order (1) creates the board and bars members with a financial or organizational interest, (2) directs a report within thirty days and (3) recites the section 10 standstill. Command (3) rests on the statute, which binds the parties for its own thirty-day period regardless of the order, so it is not an independent surviving command of the order. The order makes no appointment by name, creates no fund or property right and delegates no continuing authority, so nothing in it outlives its revocation.\",\"savings_check\":\"Savings: no savings language in EO 12553 or in this order; the completed facts are spent and nothing continues independently.\",\"scope_check\":\"Whole-order scope: EO 12553 revokes the order in full. Its table lists the order by number with no 'in part' or section limitation, so the single clause ends on the revocation date, and the held sources record no partial amendment of this order.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:11170#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:11170#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"ea93c1a5872f159728e76a8b1fd564ff8a0e55778a7b1068d218eecfedbe162c","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11170.txt","sha256":"ea93c1a5872f159728e76a8b1fd564ff8a0e55778a7b1068d218eecfedbe162c","state":"match"},"sha256":"314976d77803203796dd4e12e7d3f12a6bca8f56ac44e37dffb1dd61e9f82b4e","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11170.txt","text_sha256":"ea93c1a5872f159728e76a8b1fd564ff8a0e55778a7b1068d218eecfedbe162c"}]}]}

I read the full held Federal Register text (jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11180.txt, 29 FR 13365, PDF pages 7-9) and confirmed that its printed number, title and signature date match the census register row. The order has one operative paragraph with no numbered sections and no stated end date, so the dossier's single whole-order clause is the complete inventory (any carrier list the order attaches is an incorporated list, not a separate command). EO 12553 (signed 1986-02-25; held text jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt) revokes the order by number in its table. The first end is that express revocation. The report deadline, the report itself and the end of the standstill are not ends under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on report deadlines, and no held reviewed source establishes the dates on which the board reported or the standstill ran out. Bounded search read for this order: the dossier's three machine searches (U.S. Code release point 119-121 through 2026-10-09; eCFR and GovInfo CFR bulk through 2026-10-14; Federal Register catalog disposition notes and operative sentences of 17,521 saved order texts), after reading their coverage notes, which carry no coverage warning; the Cruden by-number scan of held order texts in the packet's LOOKUPS.md; and a full read of EO 12553 (held FR text, 33,227 characters).\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Own terms: the order states a report deadline (thirty days) and a standstill running (thirty days after the report). A report deadline is a deadline, not an end, and the standstill is a spent fact whose date no held source fixes; neither is recorded as an end. The order states no date on which it ends. Held later instruments: the by-number scan lists no held later order that cites this order other than EO 12553, which names it in its revocation table; the Federal Register catalog note records the EO 12553 revocation and the U.S. Code and eCFR searches return no hit. No unnamed statute or order was found that would be an earlier candidate end beyond section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, the statute the order itself invokes.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Independent commands: the order (1) creates the board and bars members with a financial or organizational interest, (2) directs a report within thirty days and (3) recites the section 10 standstill. Command (3) rests on the statute, which binds the parties for its own thirty-day period regardless of the order, so it is not an independent surviving command of the order. The order makes no appointment by name, creates no fund or property right and delegates no continuing authority, so nothing in it outlives its revocation.\",\"savings_check\":\"Savings: no savings language in EO 12553 or in this order; the completed facts are spent and nothing continues independently.\",\"scope_check\":\"Whole-order scope: EO 12553 revokes the order in full. Its table lists the order by number with no 'in part' or section limitation, so the single clause ends on the revocation date, and the held sources record no partial amendment of this order.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:11180#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:11180#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"8d7c93539235575c43c47d1151f354edbc2c147d271cd23e27eeed6d2d616716","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11180.txt","sha256":"8d7c93539235575c43c47d1151f354edbc2c147d271cd23e27eeed6d2d616716","state":"match"},"sha256":"718e18da0fb82cd3da39f61e5b98313942834c6b5d3351907439a80b59dd4278","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11180.txt","text_sha256":"8d7c93539235575c43c47d1151f354edbc2c147d271cd23e27eeed6d2d616716"}]}]}

I read the whole order; the representation is the machine-extracted text layer of the official PDF (OCR for this period), and I did not inspect the page images. End: express revocation by Executive Order 12553 on 1986-02-25. Searches: current-law:usc, current-law:ecfr and later-events:ofr_catalog, each marked complete in the dossier. \nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"The order states a report deadline (on or before Thursday, October 1, 1964) and a sentence that the Board shall continue in existence after its report to perform other functions required under the Act; it states no date on which the Board or the order ends. The report deadline is a deadline, not an end, and the continuing-existence sentence is the opposite of an own-terms end at the report. The held-text number search for this order found no held later order that cites it other than Executive Order 12553, and my reading of the held text of Executive Order 12553 shows no earlier revoking, superseding or terminating instrument.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Each command (create and appoint the Board, state its powers and duties by reference to Title II of the Act, set the report deadline, continue the Board in existence after the report) depends on this order and on the dispute it addressed; the powers and procedures are those of the Labor-Management Relations Act, a separate statute that does not depend on this order.\",\"savings_check\":\"Executive Order 12553 was read in full: it consists of one revoking sentence, a table of the revoked orders by number and subject, an editorial note and the signature block, and it contains no saving or transition provision. The Board's statutory functions under the Labor-Management Relations Act are those of the Act itself, not of this order, and the dispute was a one-time dispute.\",\"scope_check\":\"One clause is inventoried: the whole operative text (the recitals of the maritime disputes and threatened strike, the creation and membership of the Board of Inquiry, its powers and duties, the report deadline and the continuing-existence sentence). Executive Order 12553 revokes the order by number as a whole, so every command in the clause ends on the same date.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:11181#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:11181#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"84dd50f155be52289464d765b8371eaf74910da2864dfa00dc6c174aa934c295","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11181.txt","sha256":"84dd50f155be52289464d765b8371eaf74910da2864dfa00dc6c174aa934c295","state":"match"},"sha256":"52205bc66c833c3193ae29f140697a5e8bb053b7fbfbe33760358421d0af3aa2","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11181.txt","text_sha256":"84dd50f155be52289464d765b8371eaf74910da2864dfa00dc6c174aa934c295"}]}]}

Johnson and dated January 30, 1965; number, title and date agree with the census register. Inventory: the dossier's seven clauses (two amendment leads, four added positions and the effective-date sentence) cover every sentence of the order; I confirm them. Under section 303(g) of the Government Employees Salary Reform Act of 1964 the order amends Executive Order 11189 of November 23, 1964 by adding to its section 1 the Chief of Protocol, Department of State, as a Level IV position and to its section 2 three Level V positions (Staff Director, Commission on Civil Rights; Assistant Director (Program Planning, Analysis, and Research), Office of Economic Opportunity; General Counsel, Veterans Administration), and takes effect immediately. Ending: Executive Order 12060 (signed 1978-05-15) revokes this order by number in its section 3, and EO 11189 by the same sentence. The first end of every clause is that express revocation; amendments continue until the amended order ends (owner ruling 2026-10-21 on strike-and-insert amendments). Earlier candidate end that does not name this order, disclosed under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on unnamed instruments (option C; it is not the end because it does not name EO 11195): Pub. L. 89-554 (Sept. 6, 1966, 80 Stat. 378), which enacted title 5 of the United States Code, lists in 5 U.S.C. 5315 \"(73) Chief of Protocol, Department of State.\" and in 5 U.S.C. 5316 item (101), the Assistant Director (Program Planning, Analysis and Research), Office of Economic Opportunity, \"(110) General Counsel of the Veterans' Administration.\" and \"(114) Staff Director, Commission on Civil Rights.\", so every position this order added appears in the statute (held text jobs/source-ladder/output/texts/queue_EO_11195_Pub._L._89-554_Sept._6_1966_80_Stat._378_et_seq._including_5_U.S.C._5315_and_5316_as__c629b8a1e7.txt; it is not an evidence record of this dossier, so it is pinned here by path). The Federal Register catalog note on EO 11189 says that order was \"Nullified by 80 Stat. 460, 461 (5 U.S.C. 5315, 5316)\" because the statute included all positions added by EO 11189 and its amendment EO 11195 (jobs/later-events/output/CATALOG_INDEX.csv), but the catalog note on EO 11195 itself records only the EO 12060 revocation. Section 7(c) of the same Act provides that an order in effect under a replaced law continues in effect under the corresponding provision \"until repealed, amended, or superseded.\", which points the other way. I did not establish that the statute extinguished this order, so EO 12060 is recorded as the first end. Bounded search read for this order: the dossier's three machine searches, chosen after reading their coverage notes, which carry no coverage warning (U.S. Code release point 119-121 through 2026-10-09, whose hits for this order are the historical notes under 5 U.S.C. 5315 and 5316 and the 5 U.S.C. 5317 note stating that the order was revoked by Ex. Ord. No. 12060; eCFR and GovInfo CFR bulk through 2026-10-14, two machine hits without located text in the dossier, not relied on; Federal Register catalog disposition notes and operative sentences of 17,521 saved order texts, two machine hits, both recording the EO 12060 revocation); the number-citation scan of held order texts in the packet lookups, which found the number only in the texts of this order and of EO 12060; and a full read of EO 12060 (held Federal Register text, 1,871 characters). EO 12553 does not list 11195.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Own terms: the order says it is effective immediately and states no end. No held later order other than EO 12060 names EO 11195.
